| — Weapon description
The TH-0R Bug Taser is one of the weapons available to the Scout. It fires a chaining beam at a random enemy, dealing [ELECTRIC] damage and applying Shock to all enemies hit and to enemies who were in the path of the beams.
It can also be equipped while using Demolisher's [Class Mod Gridrunner] Gridrunner class mod.
## Weapon Mechanics and Interactions
- Additional Beams: Each additional beam increases the weapon's clip size and number of chains by one.
- Unlimited Power: Instead of each shot shooting a singular beam that jumps a certain amount of times, this overclock modifies the weapon such that each shot fires your current amount of beams 'effectively as separate shots', and each of these beam shots will subsequently jump to other enemies as the beams regularly do. When firing, the separate shots must target different bugs. If there are not enough different bugs in range, the other separate shots will dissipate (not fire).
- Single Target: If there is only a single target in range, the beams can bounce on the single target instead of bouncing to other targets.
- Jumping Beam Range: All of the jumping beams get 50% of the weapon's range.

# Vanguard Contracts
| [Drg stub] | This article is a stub. You can help Deep Rock Galactic Wiki by expanding it.
This article is a stub. You can help Deep Rock Galactic Wiki by expanding it.
[DRGS Vanguard Contract] 
Vanguard Contracts are a special type of dive that players can undertake in the game. Vanguard Contracts consist of daily missions which contain randomly assigned Mutators, having 1 beneficial and 2 negative. They can be unlocked after the player completes a dive on Hazard 3, and can be played up to Hazard 5.
## Restrictions
Vanguard Contracts can contain certain restrictions. Some restrictions include:
- Class and Class Mod
- For example, start as Demolitionist Engineer.
- Biome
- For example, Magma Core.
- Starter weapon
- For example, start with the Firefly Hunter Drone.
- Equippable weapons
- For example, can only equip [CONSTRUCT] weapons.
- Unequippable weapons
- For example, cannot equip [PROJECTILE] weapons.
- Amount of weapons
- For example, can equip up to 6 weapons.
- Early weapon unlock
- For example, you unlock weapons faster.
- Available artifacts
- For example, start with BLT Ration Pack.
- Unavailable artifacts
- For example, cannot obtain Ammo Rig.
## Completion
Every Vanguard Contract resets daily, with a live timer displaying the time remaining before reset. When completing a Vanguard Contract, the player is rewarded with [XP Gain] +200% Rank XP and [Upgrades icon] Credits as a First Win Bonus. This means that they will only receive this reward when completing it for the first time, and any further completions of the current Vanguard Contract will not provide the same reward.
Additionally, the player is guaranteed to receive an Epic piece of Gear when defeating the boss.
## Trivia
The first completion of a Vanguard Contract will grant you a guaranteed Epic piece of Gear. Since the Gear is dropped on boss death, it's possible to collect the piece of Gear, end the run by taking too much damage, and restart the dive for another piece of Gear of guaranteed quality.

| — Weapon description
The Voltaic Shock Fence is one of the weapons available to Engineer in DRG Survivor. It is unlocked by reaching class rank 3 on the Engineer.
It can also be equipped while using Demolisher's [Class Mod Gridrunner] Gridrunner class mod.
## Weapon Mechanics and Interactions
The Voltaic Shock Fence is a Turret. The Shock Fences will connect between each other through an array of electrical beams that deal [ELECTRICAL] damage and apply Shock to enemies hit. This means that stacking fence posts near or on top of each other increases their damage exponentially.
- In contrast to other [TURRET] weapons, the Shock Fence will replenish all charges after reloading, instead of replenishing them one by one. This makes Reload Speed upgrades a great choice for keeping the Shock Fences up in the field.
- A new Shock Fence cannot be placed if the player remains too close to the last placed Shock Fence. This interaction is influenced by the weapon's Weapon Range stat — meaning, the lower the Weapon Range, the shorter the distance required to place a new Shock Fence. However, even without range reductions, fences can be placed near each other by running in circles or retracing your steps — if you have moved sufficiently far from where you dropped the last fence, then you will drop a new one regardless of how many others are nearby.
- Coolant Leak: This overclock adds the [GROUNDZONE] tag to the weapon. The groundzone deals [COLD] damage and applies Slow, but the overclock does not add the [COLD] tag to the weapon.
Coolant Leak Groundzone Stats
| Stat Name | Stat | Calculation
| Damage | 2 every 0.5s/tick | Base Damage * 0.1 (rounded up)
| Lifetime | 12s | 
| Radius | 2 units | 
| Potency | 5 Slow | Base Potency * 0.5
- Disposable Tech: This overclock adds the [GROUNDZONE] and [EXPLOSION] tags to the weapon. Fences will explode at the end of their lifetime and leave an electrified groundzone.
Disposable Tech Explosion Stats
| Stat Name | Stat | Calculation
| Damage | 45 | Base Damage * 2.5
| Radius | 4 units | 
Disposable Tech Groundzone Stats
| Stat Name | Stat | Calculation
| Damage | 4 every 0.5s/tick | Base Damage * 0.2 (rounded up)
| Lifetime | 6s | 
| Radius | 3.5 units | 
| Potency | 4 Slow | Base Potency * 0.33 (rounded up)
- Magnetic Alloy: This overclock allows the Shock Fences to pick up XP and materials. The pickup radius of the Shock Fences is 3.0.
- Conduit: [CONSTRUCT] weapons with this overclock tick every 0.5 seconds and attempt to get the closest friendly [CONSTRUCT] within a 5 range radius (though it will not trigger with itself, other construct weapons are required). Once it finds a friendly [CONSTRUCT], it fires a beam between the targets that deals half of the weapon's damage, but applies the full Potency amount.
- Rapid Deployment: Instead of placing the Shock Fence charges one by one, this overclock instead makes all of the Shock Fence charges deploy at once, removing the movement requirement.
## Mastery Bonuses
Completing the Voltaic Shock Fence Weapon Mastery in Magma Core on Hazard 3+ awards the following bonuses:
[Mastery Point] +7% Damage, +7% Reload Speed

The Voltaic Stun Sweeper is one of the weapons available to Scout in DRG Survivor. It is unlocked when reaching Rank 6 on the Scout.
It can also be equipped while using Driller's [Class Mod Strong Armed] Strong Armed and Demolisher's [Class Mod Gridrunner] Gridrunner class mods.
## Weapon Mechanics and Interactions
The Voltaic Stun Sweeper is a Throwable that, much like a boomerang, fires in a straight line from the player before returning, dealing [ELECTRICAL] damage and applying Shock to enemies hit.
- The distance at which the Stun Sweeper will travel is affected by the Weapon Range stat.
- Kinda Looks Like a Magnet: This overclock allows the Stun Sweeper to pick up XP and materials. The pickup radius of the Stun Sweeper is 3.0.
- Knuckle Grip: This overclock modifies the weapon such that, instead of firing in a straight line, it will now perform a spiral move that slowly expands outwards before returning.
- One-Handed: Throws another Stun Sweeper in the opposite direction, and gives the weapon the [AKIMBO] tag.
## Mastery Bonuses
Completing the Voltaic Stun Sweeper Weapon Mastery in Salt Pits on Hazard 3+ awards the following bonus:
[Mastery Point] +12% Potency

# Wardens
Wardens do not attack, but they create a damage reduction field around them periodically, lasting until they are killed, or you stand in the radius of their purple area. They will recast this ability a short while after being disabled if not killed.
If there is space for you to enter the radius you should take the opportunity to temporarily disable their aura.
These monsters often stack up each with their own radius, making it difficult to kill them, try leading the rest of the monsters away, then returning if needed to disable the auras and kill them.
(Im unsure if the damage reduction stacks, but they do protect eachother so multiple stacked up is still a danger)

| “ | Quick firing weapon that shoots in four directionsDeals [KINETIC] damage
| — Weapon description
The Zhukov NUK17 is a weapon in DRG Survivor. It is the starting weapon of Scout's [Class Mod Recon] Recon class mod. It receives fire rate, damage, piercing, and reload speed upgrades by default.
It can also be equipped while using Gunner's [Class Mod Weapons Specialist] Weapons Specialist class mod.
## Weapon Mechanics and Interactions
The Zhukov NUK-17 shoots in four directions at once, which are 45° to the axes that the BRT7 Burst Fire Gun shoots along.
- The Explosive Reload overclock increases reload speed by 25% and adds the [EXPLOSIVE] tag, dealing damage on reload and allowing it to benefit from [EXPLOSIVE] upgrades. Notably, this is one of the few ways that Scout can naturally access Explosive Radius upgrades, which greatly benefit Cryo Grenade and Arc-Tek Cryo Guard (if the Disposable Tech Overclock is taken).
- The Death Spiral unstable overclock changes the firing pattern of the Zhukov NUK-17 allowing it to shoot in a spiralling pattern instead of fixed angles.
- The Omni Barrel unstable overclock changes the firing pattern of the Zhukov NUK-17 allowing it to fire in 8 directions.
- Equipping both the BRT7's Bullet Helix unstable overclock and the Zhukov's Omni Barrel unstable overclock unlocks the 'Eye of the Storm' achievement.
## Strategy
The Zhukov NUK-17 excels at shredding swarms through sheer projectile volume, and constant uptime rather than huge single-shot damage - this also makes it well-suited to a status effect support role, being able to apply Slow, Frozen, or Shock quickly to targets around the player with the appropriate Overclock.
However, it normally struggles against targets with high HP due to the wide distribution of its shots. Consequently, this weapon's primary role is applying damage and/or status effects to surrounding enemies, unless specialized builds are used - in this case it is also quite capable of dealing with high-HP enemies.
In all cases, in order to maximize the damage dealt by this weapon to high-value targets, close proximity is advised as the shots are more concentrated closer to the player.
- Generally, the highest-quality upgrade is the best choice, but the priority for cards of equal rarity is:
- Piercing > Damage = Fire Rate > Potency > Reload Speed
- Piercing upgrades are particularly important, as the Zhukov has a hard time penetrating far into a swarm due to its low base Piercing stat - unupgraded, the shots will be blocked almost completely by Praetorians. Note that Piercing does drop off in value once the shots are punching most or all of the way through swarms, and the point at which this happens will vary based on your Overclock and Artifact choices.
- The Zhuvok NUK-17 can also be shifted into elemental archetypes with its Overclocks. These Overclocks combine well with the Death Spiral Unstable Overclock at level 18 due to the greatly increased Fire Rate.
- The Refrigerated Gunpowder overclock makes the Zhukovs an excellent crowd control weapon, capable of quickly freezing entire screens of enemies with enough potency and piercing.
- The Battery Bullets overclock makes the Zhukovs a more damage and crit-focused weapon, and will allow it to greatly increase your crit chance against targets due to the Shock status effect. The value of Critical Damage upgrades increases drastically if you take this OC.
- If you're going to be relying on the Zhukov for killing Elites and Bosses, then taking the Omni Barrel Unstable Overclock at level 18 is advised, as it yields the highest DPS with the right positioning, due to being able to focus two bullet streams on one target in very close proximity.
- With a reasonably tanky build and/or a way to apply Frozen to targets, the Zhukov's DPS increases dramatically while standing inside a target's hitbox, because all 4 (or 8) bullet streams will hit at the same time - this gives the Zhukov the ability to quickly dispose of high-HP enemies like Elites and Bosses (just make sure you either leave the target's hitbox before it unfreezes, or you are durable enough to survive the hit).
## Mastery Bonuses
Completing the Zhukov NUK17 Weapon Mastery in Magma Core on Hazard 2+ awards the following bonus:
[Mastery Point] +12% Damage
